Use when building or redesigning a website, landing page, app screen, dashboard or component, or when asked to make a UI look better, more polished or less generic. Grounds the design in the subject, derives a direction from the subject's own world (or deals one from a library of 60 palettes, 40 type pairings and 20 layouts), locks it in DESIGN.md, builds from tokens, adds motion that explains change, and reviews with a 40-rule detector before handoff. ([Vedmeena21/ship-beautiful-ui](https://github.com/Vedmeena21/ship-beautiful-ui/tree/HEAD/skills/ship-beautiful-ui) · ★1)

- **ship-branch**: Commit the finished work and publish the branch. Use when the user says the work is done or asks to ship it. ([schubergphilis/ai-training](https://github.com/schubergphilis/ai-training/tree/HEAD/site/examples/customizing-agents/project-stays-in-charge/ship-branch) · ★4)
- **ship-branch**: Finish the current feature branch by handling dirty work safely, pushing, creating or using a PR, merging it, deleting the remote branch, switching back to the base branch, pulling the merged base, and deleting the local branch. Use when invoked with `$ship-branch`, or when the user asks to ship, finish, merge, clean up, and return to main from the current branch. ([nodatall/primedirective](https://github.com/nodatall/primedirective/tree/HEAD/skills/ship-branch) · ★2)
- **ship-branch**: Take work already committed to a branch that has no PR through the same supervised path implement-issue gives a tracked issue — open the PR with create-pr, supervise it through CI and review with bounded repairs, settle, and merge where the repository opted into auto-merge. For work implemented from a Slack thread, prose, or an issue outside an orchestrated run. A thin entry point to implement-issue's *Phase 1, from an existing branch*; it holds no rule of its own. Use when asked to ship, supervise or "put through the PR process" a branch that has no PR yet. ([baseten/agent-skills](https://github.com/baseten/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ship-branch) · ★1)
- **ship-branch**: Commit the current work, push it, open a pull request, merge it, and delete the branch locally and on the remote. Every step is skipped when it has already happened, so it is safe to re-run. ([aminurislamarnob/skills](https://github.com/aminurislamarnob/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/git/ship-branch))
- **ship-branch**: Push the current branch, open a PR into claude-design-features on the fork, merge it, and delete the remote branch. ([crywolfe/claude-code-design](https://github.com/crywolfe/claude-code-design/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ship-branch))
- **ship-branch**: Takes the current branch from done-coding to an open merge request in three gated steps — review and clean up, end-to-end browser test, then pipeline check and MR — and stops only when a gate fails or a decision belongs to the user. - **ship-branch**: Grimodex の clean な commit 済みブランチを、依頼された push、PR、merge の段階まで 進め、候補 HEAD と検証証跡を照合する。実装変更の commit 作成、version 更新、Release 公開は含めない。 ([kazormia296/Grimodex](https://github.com/kazormia296/Grimodex/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ship-branch) · 2 copies)
- **ship-branch**: Git stage of the Trello pipeline, in two modes. `spec` creates the feature branch from the base branch and commits the approved spec before any code. `code` commits the verified implementation with Spec and Trello-Card trailers, pushes the branch and confirms the commits on GitHub via gh api. Use `spec` after trello-intake and `code` after implement-spec passes. ([Kirill-jsdev/trello-pipeline](https://github.com/Kirill-jsdev/trello-pipeline/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ship-branch))
- **ship-branch**: Commit, push and open a PR the way this repo does it: feat/fix/docs/chore branch from main, explicit paths, Conventional Commits with scope = area, PR into main with an honest test plan, never merge. - **ship-branch**: Take a feature/fix branch (or current uncommitted work) safely to funnelmanager production — adversarial review → fix findings → re-review → merge to main → release → verify. Use when asked to "ship this branch", "review and deploy", "take X to prod", "land this", or after finishing a change that needs review before release. - **ship-branch**: Branch-per-change habit for git repos - one branch and one PR per unit of work, done only when the checks are green. ([tlxq/ttoolbox](https://github.com/tlxq/ttoolbox/tree/HEAD/plugins/workflow/skills/ship-branch))
